Accurate Calculation Payment Wages In November 2022 , one supplier Southeast Asia submitted grievance report allegedly properly included overtime allowance wage .
As result fact-checking respective supplier , found overtime allowance omitted due temporary error online wage management system .
The Supplier Code Conduct specifies supplier set overtime allowance higher hourly pay regular working hour pay pursuant local law regulation .
We took step supplier calculate pay unpaid allowance .
Mutual Respect among Colleagues We urge supplier build work environment ensures respect worker Supplier Code Conduct .
In April 2022 , employee engaged quarrel due miscommunication one supplier Central South America .
We conducted on-site training mutual respect human right among worker .
We received feedback issue successfully resolved plan provide continued support creating sound work environment .
Improvement On-Premise Cafeteria Services In February 2022 , one supplier Central South American submitted request improvement on-premises cafeteria service .
We confirmed employee continually filed internal complaint due inadequate amount food served .
As meal served on-premise cafeteria directly related morale employee , recommended personnel manager respective supplier seek new catering company organize tasting event .
We confirmed supplier hired new catering company took step prevent recurrence incident .

Independence Those engaged business management , holding share significant interest company , employed company within last two year eligible independent directorship .
Transparency Executive director shall appointed candidate recommended Board .
Candidates independent director shall recommended Independent Director Recommendation Committee , composed independent director .
Expertise Independent director candidate selected among knowledge experience business management , economics , accounting , law , technology , sustainability management .
Diversity The selection independent director candidate based potential ability bring diverse independent perspective Board limited candidate nationality , gender , place birth , religion area expertise .
Appointment Directors Directors appointed resolution shareholder general shareholder meeting accordance Article 382 Koreas Commercial Act .
Executive director selected among candidate recommended Board , independent director selected among candidate recommended Independent Director Recommendation Committee pursuant Article 542-8 Commercial Act .
The Article stipulates majority Independent D irector Recommendation Committee must consist independent director .
Our Independent Director Recommendation Committee consists independent director strictly abides preset candidate recommendation procedure .
Each director term office three year .
A director may reappointed general shareholder meeting upon completion initial term .
However , independent director total term limited six year pursuant Commercial Act .
Board Operation Board Convocation To convene Board meeting , Chair notifies director date , time , venue , agenda least 24 hour prior meeting pursuant Article 30 Articles Incorporation Article 8 Board Directors Regulations .
Individual director entitled request Chair convene Board meeting , agenda reason clearly defined , deemed necessary .
If Chair fails uphold responsibility without justifiable reason , respective director may directly convene Board meeting .
Board Resolution Pursuant Article 31 Articles Incorporation Article 9 Board Directors Regulations , agenda item deemed resolved attendance majority director majority vote attendee unless otherwise prescribed relevant law .
As specified Article 391 Commercial Act , director may participate Board meeting digitally using remote telecommunication device capable simultaneous voice transmission reception .
We systematically avoid potential conflict interest restricting voting right director special interest related certain agenda item pursuant Article 9 Board Directors Regulations .
Board Evaluation Independent director evaluated comprehensively across Board activity based internal criterion .
Qualitative quantitative evaluation conducted based meeting attendance rate , performance individual committee member , level expertise , understanding material issue , effort ensure independence .
The evaluation result used innovate Board operation enhance efficiency composition Board affiliated committee .
Committees Board The Board delegate authority six affiliated committee pursuant Article 28-2 Articles Incorporation Article 11-2 Board Directors Regulations ensure greater efficiency operation .

The five committee consist independent director reinforce independence .
The composition , operation , authority committee specified individual committee regulation enacted resolution Board .
Matters resolved committee notified director within two day .
Directors may request Chairman Board convene Board meeting deemed necessary , Board may reconsider revote decision made committee .
However , decision Audit Committee subject revote ensure independence .

P.20 , P.96 , P.124 CDP C1 b ) Describe management role assessing man- aging climate-related risk opportunity .

P.20 , P.96 , P.124 CDP C1 Strategy ) Describe climate-re- lated risk opportuni- tie organization identified short , medium , long term .
The risk opportunity posed climate change affecting product service , well manufacturing process , supply chain , RD , business activity .
Sam- sung Electronics monitor risk arising global operation accordance risk management process manual field , including environmental safety , climate change energy , compliance .
To understand financial impact risk opportunity arising climate change , Samsung Electronics categorizes risk factor transition risk physical risk analyzes risk opportunity arising .
In short term , see rising carbon credit price , extreme weather event , introduction high-ef- ficiency technology potential risk , securing carbon credit reducing energy cost recognized opportunity .
